# Skills · R · part 97

873 entries, review-pr to review-refute-loop. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-pr**: Triage and review a GitHub pull request for Stash. Use whenever the user says "review PR N", "check PR N", "is PR N worth merging", "should we merge N", or "handle / close PR N". First checks whether the PR's changes are already in the base branch (superseded) before doing a full review, so obsolete PRs get closed with an explanation instead of needlessly merged. Produces a clear merge / close / changes-needed verdict grounded in the repo's real state. ([stlim0727/keepory](https://github.com/stlim0727/keepory/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/review-pr) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **review-pr**: Run the local code review and the security review on a pull request with fresh-context reviewers and the spec-drift question, reviewing only the commits since the last Ready review when one passed, mark the PR ready when nothing blocks, hand it to Copilot and a cloud Auto-fix session, and post one summary comment that carries /finalize-pr's Criteria when it handed off. The only skill that marks a PR ready. Use when the user says "/review-pr", or when /review-round runs a round. Not for addressing or replying to review comments on a PR. ([collectiveai-team/botica](https://github.com/collectiveai-team/botica/tree/HEAD/skills/review/review-refute) · ★9)
- **review-refute-loop**: git diff / プラン / 直近の成果物を、実行中とは別系統のコーディングエージェント (Claude Code 実行時は Codex、Codex 実行時は Claude Code) に第三者レビューさせるスキル。依頼前にレビュー観点 (最大 5) と報告閾値を契約として固定し、観点ごとの判定と指摘理由を受け取り、各指摘をまず自分で吟味 (反証) してから、反証しきれないものだけ修正し、同じ契約のまま再レビューで収束するまで最大 3 ラウンド繰り返す。「外部レビューして」「セカンドオピニオン」「指摘を反証して」「ファクトチェックしてほしい」「この diff/プランをレビューに投げて」と言われたら発動。 ([suwa-sh/suwa-sh-claude-plugins](https://github.com/suwa-sh/suwa-sh-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/toolbox/skills/review-refute-loop))
